#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                }
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+// CUDA resources are released even if an allocation or launch fails.
+struct DeviceGrid {
+    Real* data = nullptr;
+    explicit DeviceGrid(size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "Allocating device grid");
+    }
+    ~DeviceGrid() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
+    DeviceGrid(const DeviceGrid&) = delete;
+    DeviceGrid& operator=(const DeviceGrid&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t event = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&event), "Creating timing event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { if (event) cudaEventDestroy(event); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
     }

-

-                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
-            }
-        }
-    }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(execution.stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                      "Starting stencil graph capture");
+            for (int iter = 0; iter < batch; ++iter) launchIteration(iter);
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(execution.stream, &execution.graph),
+                      "Completing stencil graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&execution.executable, execution.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                      "Instantiating stencil graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(execution.executable, execution.stream), "Uploading stencil graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(execution.stream), "Preparing stencil graph");
         }
-    }
-    
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
-    
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-    
-    // Calculate performance metrics
-    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
-    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
-    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
-

-    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
-    if (printResults) {
-        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
-    }
-

-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+        printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
+        checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.event, execution.stream), "Recording start time");
+        if (hasInterior) {
+            const int batches = batch > 0 ? iterations / batch : 0;
+            for (int b = 0; b < batches; ++b) {
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(execution.executable, execution.stream), "Launching stencil graph");
+            }
+            for (int iter = batches * batch; iter < iterations; ++iter) launchIteration(iter);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(end.event, execution.stream), "Recording end time");
+        checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(end.event), "Completing stencil computation");
+        float milliseconds = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.event, end.event), "Reading elapsed time");
+        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(milliseconds));
+
+        const double cellUpdates = hasInterior
+            ? double(nx - 2) * double(ny - 2) * double(nz - 2) * iterations : 0;
+        const double mcups = milliseconds > 0 ? cellUpdates / (double(milliseconds) * 1000.0) : 0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
+
+        // There are no host/device transfers between steps. Download once only
+        // when the caller needs the final grid for validation or result output.
+        if (printResults || validate) {
+            std::vector<Real> finalGrid(gridSize);
+            const Real* finalDevice = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1.data : grid2.data;
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), finalDevice, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                      "Downloading stencil result");
+            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+            if (validate) {
+                printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
+                printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                return valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Stencil computation failed: %s\n", error.what());
+        return 1;
     }
     
     return 0;

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+                printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
+                printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                return valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Stencil computation failed: %s\n", error.what());
+        return 1;
     }
     
     return 0;

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                // Explicit rounded division matches the CPU's double division;
+                // multiplying by an approximate reciprocal changes result bits.
+                output[idx] = __ddiv_rn(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top, 7.0);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Flattened, grid-stride tiles avoid CUDA's small Y/Z launch dimension limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz,
+                                 size_t tilesX, size_t tilesY, size_t tileCount) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tileCount; tile += gridDim.x) {
+        const size_t tx = tile % tilesX;
+        const size_t yz = tile / tilesX;
+        const size_t y = (yz % tilesY) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1;
+        const size_t x = tx * tileX + threadIdx.x;
+        const size_t firstZ = (yz / tilesY) * tileZ + 1;
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        if (x > 0 && x < nx - 1 && y < ny - 1) {
+            size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+            Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+            Real center = input[idx];
+            for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                       + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                       + bottom + top);
+                bottom = center;
+                center = top;
             }
         }
     }

codex
Profiling showed that double-precision division was the main bottleneck. A corrected reciprocal calculation preserves exact results and raised throughput from about 17 to 26 billion cell updates per second. It passed 20 million randomized arithmetic checks, and CUDA’s memory checker found no errors.
apply patch

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Grid-stride tiles also support dimensions exceeding CUDA's launch limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * tileZ + 1; firstZ < nz - 1;
+         firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * tileZ) {
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1; y < ny - 1;
+             y += size_t(gridDim.y) * tileY) {
+            for (size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x; x < nx - 1;
+                 x += size_t(gridDim.x) * tileX) {
+                if (x == 0) continue;
+                size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+                Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+                Real center = input[idx];
+                for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                    const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                    output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                           + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                           + bottom + top);
+                    bottom = center;
+                    center = top;
                 }
             }
         }

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

// CUDA resources are released even if an allocation or launch fails.
struct DeviceGrid {
    Real* data = nullptr;
    explicit DeviceGrid(size_t bytes) {
        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes), "Allocating device grid");
    }
    ~DeviceGrid() { if (data) cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceGrid(const DeviceGrid&) = delete;
    DeviceGrid& operator=(const DeviceGrid&) = delete;
};

struct CudaEvent {
    cudaEvent_t event = nullptr;
    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&event), "Creating timing event"); }
    ~CudaEvent() { if (event) cudaEventDestroy(event); }
    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
};

struct CudaExecution {
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
    CudaExecution() {
        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
                  "Creating CUDA stream");
    }
    ~CudaExecution() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    }
    CudaExecution(const CudaExecution&) = delete;
    CudaExecution& operator=(const CudaExecution&) = delete;
};

__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
                               size_t count) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
        first[i] = value;
        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
        second[i] = value;
    }
}

constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;

__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
}

// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
// Grid-stride tiles also support dimensions exceeding CUDA's launch limits.
__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * tileZ + 1; firstZ < nz - 1;
         firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * tileZ) {
        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
        for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1; y < ny - 1;
             y += size_t(gridDim.y) * tileY) {
            for (size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x; x < nx - 1;
                 x += size_t(gridDim.x) * tileX) {
                if (x == 0) continue;
                size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
                Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
                Real center = input[idx];
                for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
                    const Real top = input[idx + plane];
                    output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
                                           + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
                                           + bottom + top);
                    bottom = center;
                    center = top;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;

    const size_t maxElements = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Real);
    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
        nx > maxElements / ny || nx * ny > maxElements / nz) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Grid dimensions must be positive and fit in memory; iterations must be nonnegative.\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    try {
        const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
        const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(Real);
        DeviceGrid grid1(bytes);
        DeviceGrid grid2(bytes);
        CudaExecution execution;
        CudaEvent start;
        CudaEvent end;

        printf("Initializing grid...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(
            std::min<size_t>((gridSize - 1) / 256 + 1, 65535));
        initializeGrid<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, execution.stream>>>(grid1.data, grid2.data, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching grid initialization");
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(execution.stream), "Initializing device grids");

        const bool hasInterior = nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2;
        const size_t tilesX = (nx - 1) / tileX + 1;
        const size_t tilesY = hasInterior ? (ny - 3) / tileY + 1 : 0;
        const size_t tilesZ = hasInterior ? (nz - 3) / tileZ + 1 : 0;
        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesX, 65535)),
                          static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesY, 65535)),
                          static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(tilesZ, 65535)));
        const dim3 threads(tileX, tileY);
        const auto launchIteration = [&](int iter) {
            const Real* input = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid1.data : grid2.data;
            Real* output = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid2.data : grid1.data;
            stencilIteration<<<blocks, threads, 0, execution.stream>>>(
                input, output, nx, ny, nz);
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching stencil kernel");
        };

        // Replay batches of an even number of steps to reduce launch overhead.
        // An even batch always returns the latest values to grid1.
        const int batch = hasInterior ? std::min(iterations & ~1, 64) : 0;
        if (batch > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(execution.stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
                      "Starting stencil graph capture");
            for (int iter = 0; iter < batch; ++iter) launchIteration(iter);
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(execution.stream, &execution.graph),
                      "Completing stencil graph capture");
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&execution.executable, execution.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
                      "Instantiating stencil graph");
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(execution.executable, execution.stream), "Uploading stencil graph");
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(execution.stream), "Preparing stencil graph");
        }

        printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
        checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.event, execution.stream), "Recording start time");
        if (hasInterior) {
            const int batches = batch > 0 ? iterations / batch : 0;
            for (int b = 0; b < batches; ++b) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(execution.executable, execution.stream), "Launching stencil graph");
            }
            for (int iter = batches * batch; iter < iterations; ++iter) launchIteration(iter);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(end.event, execution.stream), "Recording end time");
        checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(end.event), "Completing stencil computation");
        float milliseconds = 0;
        checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.event, end.event), "Reading elapsed time");
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(milliseconds));

        const double cellUpdates = hasInterior
            ? double(nx - 2) * double(ny - 2) * double(nz - 2) * iterations : 0;
        const double mcups = milliseconds > 0 ? cellUpdates / (double(milliseconds) * 1000.0) : 0;
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // There are no host/device transfers between steps. Download once only
        // when the caller needs the final grid for validation or result output.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            std::vector<Real> finalGrid(gridSize);
            const Real* finalDevice = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1.data : grid2.data;
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), finalDevice, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
                      "Downloading stencil result");
            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
            if (validate) {
                printf("Validating result...\n");
                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
                printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
                return valid ? 0 : 1;
            }
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Stencil computation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }
    
    return 0;
}
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)

# Use the installed GPU by default; callers can select other architectures
# through the standard CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES cache variable.
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
endif()
project(stencil3d_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# Keep the existing source filename while compiling it with NVCC.
set_source_files_properties(stencil3d.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Preserve the reference's floating-point evaluation order and precision.
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--fmad=false;-Xcompiler=-Wall,-Wextra>)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Grid-stride tiles also support dimensions exceeding CUDA's launch limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * tileZ + 1; firstZ < nz - 1;
+         firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * tileZ) {
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1; y < ny - 1;
+             y += size_t(gridDim.y) * tileY) {
+            for (size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x; x < nx - 1;
+                 x += size_t(gridDim.x) * tileX) {
+                if (x == 0) continue;
+                size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+                Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+                Real center = input[idx];
+                for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                    const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                    output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                           + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                           + bottom + top);
+                    bottom = center;
+                    center = top;
                 }
             }
         }

best=None

    variant=re.sub(r'constexpr unsigned tileX = \\d+;',f'constexpr unsigned tileX = {tx};',source)
    variant=re.sub(r'constexpr unsigned tileY = \\d+;',f'constexpr unsigned tileY = {ty};',variant)

    times=[]
    for n,i in [(128,100),(256,50),(512,30)]:
        out=subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/stencil3d_cuda_build/stencil3d','-x',str(n),'-i',str(i)],text=True)
        times.append(float(re.search(r'Computation time: ([\\d.]+)',out).group(1)))
    score=times[0]/8.5+times[1]/32.3+times[2]/155
    print(f'tile {tx}x{ty}x{tz}: {times} ms; score {score:.4f}',flush=True)

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}
+
+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Grid-stride tiles also support dimensions exceeding CUDA's launch limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * tileZ + 1; firstZ < nz - 1;
+         firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * tileZ) {
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1; y < ny - 1;
+             y += size_t(gridDim.y) * tileY) {
+            for (size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x; x < nx - 1;
+                 x += size_t(gridDim.x) * tileX) {
+                if (x == 0) continue;
+                size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+                Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+                Real center = input[idx];
+                for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                    const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                    output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                           + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                           + bottom + top);
+                    bottom = center;
+                    center = top;
                 }
             }
         }

+__global__ void initializeGrid(Real* __restrict__ first, Real* __restrict__ second,
+                               size_t count) {
+    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         i < count; i += size_t(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const Real value = static_cast<Real>(i % 19);
+        first[i] = value;
+        // Initialize both sets of boundaries once; kernels only write interiors.
+        second[i] = value;

+constexpr unsigned tileX = 32;
+constexpr unsigned tileY = 4;
+constexpr unsigned tileZ = 8;

+__device__ __forceinline__ Real divideBySeven(Real sum) {
+    constexpr Real reciprocal = 0x1.2492492492492p-3;
+    const Real quotient = __dmul_rn(sum, reciprocal);
+    // All stencil sums are bounded, nonnegative normal doubles (or zero).
+    // The fused residual is exact here. Its correction gives the same rounded
+    // result as division by seven, without repeatedly refining a reciprocal.
+    const Real residual = __fma_rn(-7.0, quotient, sum);
+    return __fma_rn(residual, reciprocal, quotient);
+}

+// Adjacent lanes access adjacent X coordinates. Each thread streams a short
+// Z column, retaining the bottom and center values in registers between planes.
+// Grid-stride tiles also support dimensions exceeding CUDA's launch limits.
+__global__ void stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                                 Real* __restrict__ output,
+                                 size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t firstZ = size_t(blockIdx.z) * tileZ + 1; firstZ < nz - 1;
+         firstZ += size_t(gridDim.z) * tileZ) {
+        const size_t endZ = firstZ + tileZ < nz - 1 ? firstZ + tileZ : nz - 1;
+        for (size_t y = size_t(blockIdx.y) * tileY + threadIdx.y + 1; y < ny - 1;
+             y += size_t(gridDim.y) * tileY) {
+            for (size_t x = size_t(blockIdx.x) * tileX + threadIdx.x; x < nx - 1;
+                 x += size_t(gridDim.x) * tileX) {
+                if (x == 0) continue;
+                size_t idx = firstZ * plane + y * nx + x;
+                Real bottom = input[idx - plane];
+                Real center = input[idx];
+                for (size_t z = firstZ; z < endZ; ++z, idx += plane) {
+                    const Real top = input[idx + plane];
+                    output[idx] = divideBySeven(center + input[idx - 1] + input[idx + 1]
+                                           + input[idx - nx] + input[idx + nx]
+                                           + bottom + top);
+                    bottom = center;
+                    center = top;
                 }
             }
         }
